I numeri premiano Calò, non Mancini: la mancata convocazione in Nazionale fa discutere

📅 Nov 06, 2026  |  Category: Other  |  Read Source →

Nel momento esatto in cui il CT della Nazionale Roberto Mancini ha completato la lista dei 31 convocati per i prossimi impegni contro Francia e Belgio, sui social – e non solo – si è scatenata la reazione dei tifosi azzurri. Una domanda, su tutte, ha iniziato a rimbalzare con insistenza: "Ma dov'è Giacomo Calò?". Un'assenza che non è passata inosservata e che ha immediatamente acceso il dibattito tra i sostenitori della Nazionale visto l'altissimo livello mostrato finora dal centrocampista e dal Frosinone.

Numeri alla mano, nessun centrocampista tra quelli convocati da Roberto Mancini ha finora fatto meglio del 'Pittore' di Alvini. Con cinque gol segnati Calò è quinto nella classifica dei cannonieri della Serie A ma il primo, assieme a Rabiot, tra i centrocampisti. Un bottino impreziosito da un dato ancora più significativo: tutti i gol sono arrivati nelle ultime sei giornate e che lo hanno visto sempre a segno nelle ultime quattro. Uno score che racconta perfettamente lo straordinario momento di forma vissuto dal giocatore e dal Frosinone, protagonista di un mese da incorniciare.

Ma oltre al contributo offensivo, Calò è riuscito a diventare il centro nevralgico di una squadra tra le più divertenti d'Europa. Nel Frosinone il classe '97 guida la classifica delle grandi occasioni create (6), dei passaggi precisi a partita (81%), dei passaggi chiave a partita (3.8) e dei lanci lunghi (3.6). Oltre alle spiccate doti di regia, c'è poi anche un aspetto atletico da non trascurare, visto che con i suoi 10.5 km a partita soltanto il compagno di reparto Masini corre più di Calò, che ha raggiounto anche la massima velocità in sprint a 39.9 km/h.

Prestazioni che, evidentemente, non sono bastate a convincere Roberto Mancini a inserirlo nella lista dei convocati. Dal suo ritorno sulla panchina azzurra, il CT ha infatti scelto di puntare con decisione sulla linea verde, concedendo spazio e fiducia a diversi giovani talenti, anche a chi non ha ancora avuto modo di lasciare il segno nel calcio dei grandi. Una filosofia che trova la sua espressione più evidente nella convocazione di Riccardo Cassano, classe 2009 del Como, considerato uno dei prospetti più interessanti del panorama calcistico italiano, nonostante non abbia ancora collezionato nemmeno una presenza tra i professionisti. Una scelta che inevitabilmente fa discutere, soprattutto se messa a confronto con i numeri e il rendimento di Calò.

Gianluca Prestianni Becoming 'One of Best Players in Europe'

📅 Nov 06, 2026  |  Category: Sports  |  Read Source →

Rob is senior football editor at GiveMeSport, with over 15 years experience covering sport in various capacities since graduating from the University of Gloucestershire in 2008 with a degree in journalism. A childhood obsession for all things football developed into a career writing about the beautiful game, initially producing content for clients including Luton Town Football Club and the PGMOL. His first big interviews were with Premier League referees Howard Webb and Michael Oliver in 2009. He then spent four years producing written content for a betting website, mainly focusing on football, and was there to cover various games and tournaments including the 2010 World Cup and Euro 2012. Rob joined GiveMeSport in 2013 (initially as a sub-editor before becoming a senior writer and then senior editor in 2020) and has covered countless football matches and other sporting events, interviewed various high-profile figures including Rio Ferdinand, Peter Crouch and Theo Walcott, and written well over 10,000 articles during that time. Rob has played a key role in GiveMeSport's coverage of the World Cups in 2014, 2018 and 2022, plus Euro 2016, Euro 2020 and Euro 2024, along with many Champions League finals. While football is Rob's area of expertise, he has also covered a wide range of other sports during his time at GiveMeSport including boxing, tennis and darts, as well as the 2016, 2020 and 2024 Olympic Games.

Nine months have now passed since the incident that threatened to derail Gianluca Prestianni’s career.

The 20-year-old Benfica winger was accused by Real Madrid star Vinicius Jr of racially abusing him during a Champions League knockout play-off clash in February.

The match at Benfica’s Estadio da Luz in Lisbon was halted for 10 minutes before play eventually resumed.

Prestianni vehemently denied Vini Jr’s accusation, but the situation was made more complex due to the Argentine covering his mouth.

Jose Mourinho, Benfica’s manager at the time, said Prestianni’s career under him would be “over” if the abuse was proven.

Two weeks after the incident, FIFA president Gianni Infantino said players who cover their mouths while speaking to opponents during confrontations should be sent off. The so-called ‘Prestianni rule’ was subsequently made official ahead of the 2026 World Cup finals in North America.

Prestianni was eventually given a six-game ban by UEFA for homophobic conduct, rather than racist abuse, which would have carried a minimum 10-match ban for European competition.

Nevertheless, the outcome still threatened to cast a permanent shadow over Prestianni’s character and future opportunities in the game.

“The result all feels a bit unsatisfactory,” BBC Sport’s football issues correspondent Dale Johnson wrote in April. “Prestianni was accused of making a racist remark, which carries a mandatory 10-game ban. But he will serve half of a six-match ban for using a homophobic slur.

“Despite admitting it. And despite causing a Champions League game to be suspended, with the images beamed around the globe.

“It gives the impression that homophobic abuse is being treated far less seriously than racism. Which, culturally in football, it always has been.”

Fast-forward to November 2026 and Prestianni has put last season’s incident behind him and is rapidly cementing his status as one of the best young wingers in Europe.

In 19 appearances for Benfica this season, the winger has registered 11 goals and provided six assists.

He scored a stunning solo goal against Lech Poznan in the Europa League on Thursday night, leading some to claim it won’t be long before “Prestianni is considered one of the best players in Europe”.

Capped three times by the Argentina senior team, Prestianni looks set to play a major role for the three-time world champions as they begin life without the legendary Lionel Messi, who called time on his glittering international career in August.

With Como’s excellent playmaker Nico Paz also impressing everyone during the opening months of the 2026-27 campaign, it appears that he new-look Argentina national team will continue to pose a major threat in future tournaments despite Messi’s departure.

Malaysia: Jailed ex-leader Najib to leave prison for house arrest

📅 Nov 06, 2026  |  Category: Business  |  Read Source →

Convicted in the 1MDB scandal for embezzling millions, jailed former Prime Minister Najib Razak will serve his prison term under house arrest after his party had collected donations to pay his fine.

Malaysia's graft-convicted former Prime Minister Najib Razak is set to leave prison to sit out the remainder of a six-year sentence under house arrest, after his political party paid a 50 million ringgit ($12.3 million, €11 million) fine.

Najib's party, United Malays National Organization (UMNO), collected sufficient funds through a donation drive, Asyraf Wajid Dusuki, the party secretary-general, said in a statement on Friday.

Although it was not immediately clear when Najib would be officially allowed to leave prison, the AFP news agency said a report sa him in a car driving away on Friday. Police were stationed at his house in Kuala Lumpur early Friday and the road leading to his home was blocked.

Najib, 73, has been serving a six-year prison sentence for his role in the 1MDB corruption scandal, named after 1Malaysia Development Berhad, a state investment fund he co-founded while serving as prime minister from 2009 to 2018.

In 2020, Najib was originally sentenced to 12 years in prison for abuse of power, criminal breach of trust and money laundering involving $10.3 million funneled into his bank accounts. He began his sentence in 2022.

Malaysia's king, Sultan Ibrahim, granted Najib a rare royal clemency in September, subject to the payment of 50 million Malaysian ringgit, allowing him to serve the rest of his sentence under house arrest.

The house arrest applies only to his reduced six-year sentence in the first trial.

It doesn't cover a separate 15-year prison sentence imposed in December 2025 for corruption and money laundering in a second trial tied to the 1MDB scandal.

To view this video please enable JavaScript, and consider upgrading to a web browser that supports HTML5 video

Najib is appealing that conviction, which saw him fined 11.4 billion ringgit.

If he fails to win the appeal, he could go back to prison after August 23, 2028.

The scandal sparked national outrage in Malaysia and led to the 2018 election defeat of Najib's party United Malays National Organization (UNMO), which governed the country since independence from Britain in 1957.

In spite of the embezzlement, Najib still enjoys political support and is influential in this party, which is part of the government that came to power in 2022.

Kalshi hit with injunction over sports contracts on California tribal lands

📅 Nov 06, 2026  |  Category: Technology  |  Read Source →

Get The Block’s latest crypto news and price alerts for the coins you follow, right in Chrome. Add our free extension here.

A federal judge has granted a preliminary injunction barring Kalshi from offering sports-event contracts on the lands of Blue Lake Rancheria and the Chicken Ranch Rancheria of Me-Wuk Indians.

A federal judge has granted a preliminary injunction barring Kalshi from offering sports-event contracts on the lands of two California tribes.

In a Wednesday order, U.S. District Judge Jacqueline Scott Corley ruled that the injunction covers contracts tied to sports including football, basketball, baseball, soccer, tennis, and Ultimate Fighting Championship events.

The tribes — Blue Lake Rancheria and the Chicken Ranch Rancheria of Me-Wuk Indians — must provide boundary maps by Nov. 13, the judge said.

Kalshi is required to report the status of the steps it has taken to comply with the injunction within 21 days, according to the order.

Sports and gaming attorney Daniel Wallach described the order as the first preliminary injunction secured by tribes against Kalshi nationwide, calling it "a major development in tribal gaming litigation."

The tribes filed the lawsuit in July 2025, alleging that Kalshi's sports event contracts constitute unauthorized Class III gaming under the Indian Gaming Regulatory Act (IGRA) and violate the tribes' gaming ordinances.

Kalshi has argued that its sports event contracts are federally regulated swaps under the Commodity Exchange Act (CEA), and that the CEA's grant of exclusive jurisdiction to the Commodity Futures Trading Commission preempts tribal gaming law.

The judge initially denied the tribes' motion for a preliminary injunction in November 2025. The tribes then appealed, and a Ninth Circuit panel partially reversed the decision in September.

"Reversing the district court's denial of a preliminary injunction in part, the panel held that the tribes were likely to succeed in their claims that Kalshi's sports event contracts on tribal lands violated IGRA and the tribes' gaming ordinances," the panel wrote.

The Ninth Circuit panel also held that the CEA's "exclusive" jurisdiction did not extend to Kalshi's sports event contracts.

The Block has reached out to Kalshi for comment.

Kalshi faces mounting legal challenges, with more states taking action against the prediction markets platform.

On Wednesday, Arkansas Attorney General Tim Griffin sued Kalshi for allegedly operating an illegal gambling platform in the state.

"It doesn't take a genius to understand that wagering money on whether the Razorbacks win or lose is gambling," Griffin said, adding that such practice is "squarely prohibited" by Arkansas law.

Last week, Utah also filed criminal charges against Kalshi and two company executives, accusing them of illegally promoting online gambling. Utah has also sued Kalshi and two Susquehanna trading firms for allegedly operating an illegal gambling business.

Napoli in ritiro verso la Lazio: Allegri mantiene la fiducia del club

📅 Nov 06, 2026  |  Category: Other  |  Read Source →

La decisione di De Laurentiis arriva dopo il 4-1 subito dal Porto e la settima sconfitta in quattordici gare.

Il Napoli andrà in ritiro da questa sera per preparare la partita di domenica contro la Lazio di Rino Gattuso. Secondo quanto riportato da Il Messaggero, Aurelio De Laurentiis ha deciso di intervenire dopo il 4-1 subito sul campo del Porto. Per il momento non sono previsti cambi in panchina: Max Allegri conserva la fiducia della società.

CLICCA QUI E SEGUI LAZIOPRESS.IT ANCHE SU INSTAGRAM

Alla sconfitta in Portogallo è seguito un giorno di silenzio, dopo le dichiarazioni rilasciate allo stadio Dragao. Il Napoli ha perso sette delle prime quattordici gare stagionali. Contro il Porto si è presentato con un gruppo ridotto dai numerosi infortuni e con i nervi tesi per i risultati.

Le parole di Politano hanno mostrato le difficoltà dello spogliatoio: «Vorrei che la faccia non ce la mettessimo soltanto noi italiani». La squadra fatica a ritrovare compattezza e Allegri, a sua volta, è finito al centro delle critiche.

Secondo Il Messaggero, il Napoli non ha dato una sensazione di solidità e spesso è calato dopo un tempo di gioco. È accaduto anche al di là dell’emergenza infortuni, destinata a proseguire nella sfida con i biancocelesti.

De Laurentiis è infuriato. In estate aveva sostenuto la forza dell’organico e la necessità di intervenire poco sul mercato, confidando nella qualità della rosa. A distanza di pochi mesi, diversi titolari attraversano invece un periodo difficile.

Rrahmani appare lontano dal rendimento autorevole delle ultime due stagioni. Di Lorenzo, ora assente, Politano e Anguissa hanno avuto prestazioni altalenanti, così come Kevin De Bruyne. Il belga suscita perplessità anche sotto il profilo caratteriale: si espone poco nel ruolo di leader e resta uno degli interrogativi della squadra.

La società non pensa, al momento, a un ribaltone tecnico, ma ha scelto il ritiro per tutto il gruppo come segnale in vista della Lazio. Il Napoli è rientrato dal Portogallo soltanto ieri pomeriggio; i confronti interni sono stati rinviati a oggi. Ad Allegri spetterà riportare tranquillità in un ambiente deluso. Mercoledì sera i tifosi hanno contestato la squadra al Dragao e al Maradona si attende una reazione.

Equinix Opens SG6 in Singapore, Expanding AI-Ready Capacity in One of Asia-Pacific's Most Connected Markets

📅 Nov 06, 2026  |  Category: Technology  |  Read Source →

Latest data center combines liquid cooling, renewable energy and global connectivity to support next-generation computing demands

REDWOOD CITY, Calif., Nov. 6, 2026 /PRNewswire/ -- Equinix, Inc. (Nasdaq: EQIX), the world's digital infrastructure companyÂ®, today announced the opening of SG6, its sixth International Business Exchangeâ¢ (IBXÂ®) data center in Singapore. Designed to support AI, high-performance computing and data-intensive workloads, SG6 expands Equinix's global footprint of more than 280 data centers and provides customers with the infrastructure needed to connect, innovate and scale in an increasingly AI-driven economy.

As businesses worldwide accelerate investments in AI, cloud and digital services, demand is growing for highly connected infrastructure that can support next-generation applications while advancing sustainability objectives. Located in one of Asia-Pacific's most strategically connected markets, SG6 combines private interconnection, global ecosystems and advanced infrastructure to help organizations deploy distributed digital workloads across regions.

"Singapore sits at the intersection of global commerce, digital technology and sustainable development," said Adaire Fox-Martin, Chief Executive Officer and President, Equinix. "Equinix has been investing in Singapore for nearly 25 years, and our new facility will further expand our presence as the neutral exchange where enterprises connect to the clouds, networks and partners they need to innovate and grow. Singapore is one of our most important markets globally, and our continued investment here is a testament to the central role it plays in the digital economy."

SG6 is among the first data centers developed under Singapore's Data Centre Call for Application framework and is designed to support high-density AI deployments through advanced cooling technologies, including proprietary cooling systems and liquid-cooling capabilities that accommodate power-intensive GPU infrastructure.

The opening of SG6 further advances Equinix's Future First sustainability strategy and aligns with the company's science-based commitment to achieve net zero emissions by 2040 and 100% renewable energy coverage globally by 2030 across its retail IBXs data centers. Equinix's Singapore data centers have maintained 100% renewable energy coverage since 2020. SG6 is designed to achieve a Power Usage Effectiveness (PUE) of 1.20 and is targeting Green Mark Platinum certification. The facility also incorporates rooftop and facade solar photovoltaic systems, rainwater harvesting capabilities and lower-embodied-carbon construction materials to support more efficient operations and responsible growth.

"Asia-Pacific is at the forefront of digital transformation, with AI adoption, cloud expansion and data-intensive workloads driving unprecedented demand for high-performance digital infrastructure," said Cyrus Adaggra, President, Asia-Pacific, Equinix. "Singapore remains a strategic gateway for businesses serving the region, connecting key markets, talent and innovation ecosystems. SG6 strengthens our ability to support customers as they scale across Asia-Pacific, providing the resilient, interconnected and sustainable infrastructure they need to accelerate innovation and capture new growth opportunities."

With an initial investment of approximately $290 million, SG6 provides 8 MW of capacity in its first phase, delivering approximately 1,550 cabinets. Upon full build-out, the facility will offer up to 20 MW of IT capacity.

Equinix also plans to open a Co-Innovation Facility (CIF) at SG6 in partnership with the National University of Singapore in 2027. The facility will focus on advancing technologies that improve data center sustainability and energy efficiency, including liquid cooling, cognitive digital twins and battery energy storage systems.

Since entering Singapore in 2002, Equinix has expanded from a single data center to six facilities, creating one of Asia-Pacific's most densely interconnected digital infrastructure hubs. Equinix's Singapore operations connect enterprises to a rich ecosystem of cloud, IT, financial services, communications, media and technology providers, while supporting connectivity to global markets through more than 17 subsea cable systems.

About EquinixEquinix, Inc. (Nasdaq: EQIX) shortens the path to boundless connectivity anywhere in the world. Its digital infrastructure, data center footprint and interconnected ecosystems empower innovations that enhance our work, life and planet. Norwich City face 'no-brainer' Anthony Musaba transfer call on one major condition

📅 Nov 06, 2026  |  Category: Sports  |  Read Source →

Norwich City have found some form after a difficult start to the new Championship season.

Philippe Clement’s side went into the campaign hoping to compete for promotion to the Premier League after a strong end to 2025/26.

And the Canaries are now starting to show why many tipped them for a top eight finish, with wins against Derby County, Watford and Charlton Athletic.

And the emergence of summer signing Anthony Musaba has been central in that uptick in form, with the winger scoring in both of their last two league victories.

The 25-year-old arrived on loan from Fenerbahce, and is starting to become an integral part of Clement’s first team plans.

But it remains to be seen whether a permanent move could be on the cards in the future, with the Dutchman previously struggling for game time with the Turkish giants.

When asked about the prospect of completing a permanent move for Musaba at the end of this loan spell, FLW’s Norwich fan pundit Zeke Downes claimed that he is the first proper winger the team have had in quite some time, potentially since Chris Hughton was in charge.

However, he believes it is still too early to suggest a permanent deal should be sought, and that any decision will have to wait until we see where the team ends up at the end of the campaign.

“I really like Musaba, I think he’s a proper winger, and we don’t have many of those at the club, and we’ve not had many of those really for a long time,” Downes told Football League World.

“Since maybe Hughton, around that time, because even when we were in the Premier League more recently, we had players like Buendia and Cantwell, and they’re not really wingers.

“So, it’s nice to have a proper winger that’s actually going to run with the ball, and take players on, and have a go from distance.

“It’s a really big help having him in the team, and I like that he is now able to get that game time that he’s not really had yet, and he’s proving his worth.

Should Norwich City pursue a permanent move for Anthony Musaba? Comment your opinion below

Musaba signed for Fenerbahce last January from Samsunspor, but he started just 10 of his 17 league appearances for the club in the second half of the campaign.

That opened the door for him to go out on loan in the summer, and the 25-year-old has so far started six of his 12 appearances in the Championship.

Goals in victories against Watford and Derby County this week has shown what he can bring to the team, and he could now be in line to become a consistent presence in the starting lineup going forward.

Norwich have climbed to 12th in the Championship table with three wins in a row in their last three fixtures.

The Canaries are now just five points behind eighth place West Ham, with their sights set on earning a top eight finish this year.

Next up for the Norfolk outfit is a trip to face Bristol City at Ashton Gate on 7 November in a 12.30pm kick-off.

Musaba previously proved what he can do at a Championship level when he was at Sheffield Wednesday, but he made a slow start to life at Carrow Road.

But the last week has shown what he can bring to the team, and goals against Watford and Derby could be a precursor to him going on a strong run again.

If he can maintain his recent performance levels, then it would be a no-brainer decision for Norwich to pursue a permanent move.

But it is perhaps still too early to make any definitive statements, as it is still only November, and there are plenty of games still to be played this season.

Of ‘Black Sea Waves’ and ‘gummy bears’: Freiburg’s Julian Schuster explains Conference League triumph

📅 Nov 06, 2026  |  Category: Sports  |  Read Source →

SC Freiburg head coach Julian Schuster has offered an unusual explanation for his team’s impressive 1-0 Conference League victory over Trabzonspor on Thursday evening. The 41-year-old trainer compared the tactical discipline required to overcome the Turkish side to children being told not to touch a tempting supply of Haribo gummy bears.

Freiburg secured their second Conference League victory through a 14th-minute goal from Lukas Kübler, successfully protecting their narrow advantage despite increasing pressure from the hosts after halftime.

The victory represented an important response to Freiburg’s recent difficulties against stronger opposition. Defeats against Bayer Leverkusen and Bayern Munich in the Bundesliga, along with a disappointing 0-3 loss to AS Monaco in the Conference League, had raised questions about the Schwarzwaldverein’s ability to compete consistently against higher-calibre opponents.

Thursday’s performance provided an encouraging answer.

Trabzonspor entered the encounter with considerable momentum, having recorded seven consecutive victories under German head coach Thomas Reis. The Turkish outfit’s aggressive pressing and dangerous counterattacking play presented a significant challenge for Freiburg.

Schuster’s side nevertheless took the initiative early on, repeatedly forcing mistakes in Trabzonspor’s buildup through determined pressing of their own. As detailed in Get German Football News’ firsthand match report and player ratings, Freiburg created several opportunities during an impressive opening period before Kübler capitalized on André Onana’s spilled save to put the visitors ahead.

The Bundesliga representatives subsequently demonstrated an increasingly mature understanding of when to attack and when to exercise restraint. Speaking after the match, Schuster praised the precision with which his players executed their instructions.

Schuster was particularly impressed by the team’s ability to resist tempting attacking opportunities when a loss of possession could have left them vulnerable to Trabzonspor’s dangerous transitions.

“The lads really carried out all the assignments we gave them to perfection,” the Freiburg coach explained. “Whenever there’s something tempting, you want to reach for it, but you mustn’t give in. I’m thinking of an example involving children and Haribo gummy bears. They’re sitting alone in a room and are told not to touch them.”

The Freiburg trainer emphasized that resisting such opportunities was particularly demanding from a psychological perspective.

Schuster’s observations also reflected the broader development of a team that has experienced considerable success during his tenure without always managing to impose itself against elite opposition. The difficulties in Monaco provided a particularly useful comparison. Freiburg had struggled to establish themselves in that contest, eventually returning from the principality with a three-goal defeat.

The circumstances in Trabzon could hardly have been more different.

In addition to the hosts’ aggressive approach, Freiburg were confronted by a particularly hostile atmosphere. Rather than allowing the conditions to unsettle them, Freiburg used the atmosphere as additional motivation.

The visitors also demonstrated considerable defensive resolve after Trabzonspor increased the pressure during the second half. Jordy Makengo, Philipp Lienhart, Matthias Ginter, and Lukas Kübler worked effectively as a defensive unit, while goalkeeper Mio Backhaus produced a crucial intervention to deny Mohamed Salah an equalizer.

Schuster drew a colourful comparison between the Turkish coastline and the waves of pressure his side encountered after halftime. During a team meeting, the Freiburg coach had looked out at the Black Sea and noticed that the waves differed considerably from those he had observed along the Mediterranean coast.

“Perhaps that was symbolic of some of the waves that came at us in the second half,” Schuster explained. “The lads stood firm against them and let everything bounce off. That’s quite remarkable.”

The Freiburg coach ultimately described the performance as his team’s best of the season. The result leaves Freiburg with six points from their opening three Conference League fixtures and strengthens their prospects of advancing directly to the Round-of-16.

More importantly, it offered another indication of the progress Schuster continues to demand from his squad. As previously demonstrated during Freiburg’s ten-man Bundesliga victory over Schalke, the Sport-Club have increasingly shown an ability to adjust to difficult circumstances without abandoning their collective principles.

In Trabzon, that maturity meant recognizing that not every promising opportunity was worth pursuing.

Sometimes, as Schuster’s unusual comparison suggested, the smartest decision is simply to leave the gummy bears alone.

Amorim a CBS: "Questa partita ha detto a me e a tutti che non siamo pronti per essere una squadra molto forte"

📅 Nov 06, 2026  |  Category: Sports  |  Read Source →

Ruben Amorim, allenatore del Milan, è stato intervistato da CBS Sports Golazo al termine di Milan-Ferencvaros (0-2) di Europa League.

Ieri parlavi di identità, di carattere. È stata questa la differenza con il Ferencvaros?

"Sì, tecnicamente, tatticamente e soprattutto mentalmente sono stati superiori alla partita e hanno meritato di vincere. Oggi non eravamo pronti a giocare a calcio a livello professionistico, e l'ho avvertito. Dobbiamo guardare dentro noi stessi e fare di meglio".

E in Champions League il livello sarebbe ancora più alto...

"Dobbiamo concentrarci sui piccoli dettagli, senza pensare alla Champions League o a cose simili. Dobbiamo solo focalizzarci sul miglioramento degli aspetti più specifici del calcio: la mentalità, il cuore, la passione. Dobbiamo migliorare in questo".

Qual è il livello del Ferencvaros nel calcio europeo, come lo valuti?

"Non lo so, dipende dall'avversario, ma stanno facendo benissimo. Hanno quattro vittorie in quattro partite, un'identità chiara e oggi sono stati superiori a noi".

Cosa ti ha detto questa partita sul Milan?

"Che non siamo pronti. Non siamo pronti, siamo lontani dall'essere una squadra davvero forte. Abbiamo dei momenti, in alcune partite, in cui siamo davvero forti, ma poi non riusciamo a mantenere il livello, soprattutto a livello mentale. Quindi questa partita ha detto a me e a tutti noi che non siamo pronti per essere una squadra molto forte".

Durante il primo tempo ho notato che eri molto energico a bordo campo, e poi è sembrato quasi che tu abbia smesso di dare indicazioni. Si può dire che ci sia una certa mancanza di intesa con la squadra e che non stiano giocando nel modo in cui vorresti?

"No, faccio semplicemente quello che sento in quel momento, e a volte non faccio determinati gesti solo per fare scena. Non abbiamo giocato bene, questo è certo; abbiamo avuto un atteggiamento completamente diverso nell'ultima partita di campionato, mentre oggi non eravamo mentalmente preparati a lottare per la vittoria, ed è su questo che dobbiamo lavorare".

Se non ci fosse stato il derby nel fine settimana, pensi che questa partita sarebbe andata meglio?

"Non ne ho idea, non importa. Non conta, dobbiamo essere pronti a giocare ogni tre giorni in competizioni diverse. Quindi non importa se avevamo una partita prima o no. Credo che dobbiamo migliorare tantissimo in tutti gli aspetti del gioco".

Torriani ha giocato la sua prima partita da titolare. Come valuti la sua prestazione oggi?

"Penso che abbia fatto bene nel primo tempo. Ha avuto un momento difficile su un passaggio, ma Lorenzo non ha alcuna responsabilità per la prestazione della squadra. Anzi, nel primo tempo ci ha dato il tempo di provare a cambiare qualcosa nella ripresa. Quindi Lorenzo deve solo continuare a lavorare. Dobbiamo migliorare come squadra per essere più forti".

One single word which proves the Nuno era is truly dead and buried | West Ham News

📅 Nov 06, 2026  |  Category: Sports  |  Read Source →

Many fans feel they saw the very best, and the worst of West Ham United in the ‘game of two halves’ that unfolded before our eyes on Wednesday night.

Cardiff City’s threadbare, increasingly tiring squad no doubt helped the Hammers along the way, but the starting mindset seemed to sum up everything that was wrong with Nuno Espírito Santo’s West Ham  performances of late, and the second half recovery hopefully signposts what is to come under a less cautious Scott Parker.

One word stands out amongst all of the media interviews and comments made by the newly appointed head coach and his players after the game, which differentiates vividly the two managers’ styles. And hopefully points towards a fundamental shift now that Nuno is no longer.

High risk football: Nuno wasn’t capable of taking off the hand brake

As reported in the standard.co.uk, Scott Parker explained what he’d addressed at half time in the Cardiff dressing room.:

“Certainly out of possession, we needed to be more aggressive.“

There were tactical changes and substitutions, and we became more front-footed.

If any one word sums up the failure of the Nuno era it is that word: Risk. The cautious Portuguese manager cost the Hammers their Premier League place by trying to defend leads, throwing on defensive substitutes in frequent, vain hopes of keeping hold of a lead – and far too often set the team up to hang on rather than to score more.

And it was his insistence on doing the same this season, rather than setting out to dominate the division, that was driving home supporters to distraction and in the end cost him his job.

Whatever else, if only from from the first, brief but encouraging impressions of one half of football and from his words after, Scott Parker seems more prepared for the higher-risk football that goes with winning and dominating a campaign in this division.

His arrival was heralded by the moaners as the landing of ‘another negative coach’: Its of course early days, but that might prove to be a long way from the truth.

From the old Bobby Moore Upper to the Billy Bonds' stand these days - I've been watching since '03 and a supporter since about 1970.. Favourite player - Dean Ashton: Curse you Shaun Wright-Phillips. I still watch YouTube repeats of the Cup Final of 2006 hoping in vain that Shaka Hislop grows another six inches and stops Steven Gerrard's injury time equaliser. Can tell I'm getting old knowing I watched both Mark Noble's debut and his last game at West Ham. Pulling on a Claret and Blue replica shirt still makes me feel the same butterflies as when I was seven years old. Magic.

Borussia Dortmund vs. SV Elversberg preview: Contrasting styles sets up epic clash

📅 Nov 06, 2026  |  Category: Health  |  Read Source →

With the final international break of 2027 just around the corner, Borussia Dortmund will be keen to go into the imposed rest on a high this weekend. On Saturday afternoon at 15:30 CET, they await the arrival of a newly promoted outfit SV Elversberg on Matchday 9.

Dortmund managed to use last weekend's impressive 2-2 stalemate away to Bayern Munich in their favour midweek. A dominant yet momentarily jittery performance enabled them to defeat Real Betis on a 2-1 scoreline in Wednesday's Champions League encounter.

The result appeared to come at a slight cost, however, for Niko Kovač's troops. Serhou Guirassy hobbled off, leaving him as a doubt for Saturday. Nonetheless, Fábio Silva will be ready to deputise having bagged yet another goal against their Andalusian opponents.

Perhaps even more confidence-building is the fact Kostas Karetsas also scored. The Greek talent has been blighted by injuries, but opening the account will likely give his belief a major boost. BVB's solid rearguard also remains the best in the league, leaking just six.

Serhou Guirassy (strain) could be fit in time, likewise Marcel Sabitzer (illness). Emre Can and Giannis Konstantelias (both ACL tears) naturally won't feature, while Justin Lerma (muscle injury) is expected to remain out.

As for the visitors, this is uncharted territory for SV Elversberg. They ride into town where they will get a taste of one of the Bundesliga's finest footballing temples. The biggest task thereafter is becoming quickly acclimatised to the atmosphere that often unnerves sides.

It's no surprise that this is the first meeting of these two club's in Germany's topflight. The Saarland outfit's meteoric rise up the divisions presents an exciting clash. In ways, it's like a throwback given the visitors play a style akin to Jürgen Klopp's heavy metal era at BVB.

A lot of that credit has to go to coach Vincent Wagner who, along with the Elversberg's chiefs, said they will show no fear and go after teams. That toe-to-toe battling style may just be exactly what could catch Dortmund cold on Saturday if they can make a fast start.

Form has been somewhat turbulent in recent weeks for Elversberg. A crushing DFB-Pokal exit to SV Darmstadt (2-0) and thumping loss to RB Leipzig (4-0) left scars. Fortunately, they bounced back with a barnstorming 4-1 victory over Mainz to settle the nerves last Friday.

Elversberg have a relatively clean bill of health aside from Jan Gyamerah (muscle), Luis Seifert (ligament tear), Tom Zimmerscheid (ankle) and Noah Darvich (muscular problems) who are all due to miss this game.

📅 Nov 06, 2026  |  Category: Other  |  Read Source →

It's the seventh top 10 appearance, and third leader, for Josh Homme and Co.

It’s a perfecth start for Queens of the Stone Age on the ARIA Albums Chart, as the U.S. rock act debuts at No. 1.

QOTSA leads the latest chart with Perfecth, their ninth studio album. It’s the seventh top 10 appearance for Josh Homme and Co., and their third leader after 2013’s …Like Clockwork and 2017’s Villains. Perfecth is the best seller on wax for the week, earning top spot on ARIA’s Vinyl Chart.

Canadian rock favorites Nickelback start on the chart podium with Everything Under The Sun, their 11th studio album. It’s new at No. 3. Nickelback now have 10 ARIA top 10 albums, including a best-seller with 2011’s Here And Now, which logged one week at No. 1. Australian actor, singer and comedian Eddie Perfect enjoys a career-best result with Beetlejuice (Live At The Regent Theatre Melbourne), which opens its account at No. 5 on the latest chart, published Friday, Nov. 6. Indeed, it’s a first-ever appearance in the top 50 for Perfect, who reprised the title role last year in the Australian production of Beetlejuice The Musical.

Close behind is Aussie country artist Fanny Lumsden, whose new album Traditions debuts at No. 6 on the ARIA Chart. That’s Lumsden’s third consecutive top 10 appearance and a new career high. Traditions is the fifth studio album for Lumsden, a two-time ARIA Award winner.

Gretta Ray has her own career high with Heartsong, the Melbourne-born singer and songwriter’s third studio album. It’s new at No. 11, besting the peak of her 2021 debut Begin To Love Around, and the No. 17 best for 2023’s Positive Spin. It’s an uplifting comeback for Ray, who endured a five-week hospital stay in 2024 after falling ill with “severe” cardiac disease — infective endocarditis, a rare and life-threatening ailment that can cause damage to the heart.

Also new to the chart is U.S. singer Dominic Fike’s third LP How To Quit Smoking, new No. 22. That bests the No. 34 for his 2020 collection What Could Possibly Go Wrong and the No. 25 peak for 2023’s Sunburn.

Over on the ARIA Singles Chart, Tate McRae enters a third consecutive week at No. 1 with “Housewife,” and there’s a post-Halloween bump for Tame Impala’s “Dracula,” which rises 20-13.

Investing - Theory, News &amp; General • Re: Box Spreads as Loans - Interactive Brokers IBKR - 2021 [and later]

📅 Nov 06, 2026  |  Category: Other  |  Read Source →

If you allow a box to expire, you will get "perfect" execution. If you close a box prior to expiration, you will be exposed to the bid-ask spread. This may or may not be important to you. I tend to open new positions on expiration day. But if you are not sitting in front of a computer every day, and you can't guarantee that you will be available to conduct a roll-forward on expiration day, it may make more sense to roll your positions ahead of time.
I think that is correct, and if you wait until relatively close to expiration day with rolling, then you are likely to pay (on average) half the bid/ask spread of 0.05 points, which equates to a dollar value $2.50, plus commissions. A few weeks before, you might pay 2-3 times the minimum tick size, based on my observations of the SyntheticFi quotes.

Statistics: Posted by comeinvest — Fri Nov 06, 2026 2:25 am
